Branches reconnect, are time-compensated/loss-proof/replayable, never gate S/reveal/Stage 8/boss/base Museum, and Cheat-Assisted gives content only with spoiler-safe locked clues.
- **Confirmed:** Stage 7 solo/co-op is **One Investigation, Shared Authority**: identical route/encounters/boss/ledgers/witnesses/branches/gates/robot/resources/outcome. Solo uses one hero/channel/bike with compensated sequential timing; co-op two unique heroes, two staggered separate channels/two bikes but no extra objectives/gates, scaling coverage not Health. All mission state is once-only host authority, any hero completes objectives, Kael is never required, camera/regroup/down-window/rescue rules preserve fairness. Separate normalized records and informational contributions apply; shared rewards/content never require co-op and unselected Kael grants no advantage.
- **Confirmed:** Stage 7 uses five anchors: Forward Office Departure, Consultancy Secured, Freight Exchange Secured, Public Wing Cleared and Three Keys Authenticated; no mid-Silas checkpoint, exact final-anchor restart. Full hero/evidence/witness/branch/vehicle/property/robot/enemy/boss/score/network/package state persists without improvement/reroll/duplication/reversal. Validated recovery and one consumed suspension preserve eligibility; Continue retains story/Stage 8 only, Restart is fresh. Disconnect standby plus reconnect/migration before play preserves class; solo continuation marks Party Changed, safely withdraws absent state and retains access/records but no competitive/economy/achievement/prestige eligibility, which rejoin cannot restore.
- **Confirmed:** Stage 7 uses a 100-point Evidence and Protection Grade: evidence/authentication 35 (ledger 10/7/4 plus 5), witnesses 25 (group 8/5/2 plus 1), combat 20, property 10, resource/time 10; C valid, B60, A75, S90. Valid requires three authenticated ledgers, all groups safe, live Silas. S needs two Preserved/third Reconstructed+, all groups Protected/no Damaged property; Essential Core/emergency/property/purge caps are explicit. Branches add five post-rank points each with time compensation, never gate S. Access time is excluded and farming decays; ineligible classes keep completion/access only.
- **Confirmed:** Stage 7 has eight localized eligible achievements for clear, each/both branches, all-ledger Preserved, all mandatory groups Protected, all property Protected and zero Silas purge steps plus live capture. Any canonical clear unlocks Stage 8/Chapter Challenge/mandatory records/media; first eligible clear gives Credits/outfit visibility, A the Three Linked Keys emblem, S Guardian of the Record, Ultimate Witness to the Truth. Stage 7 activates Soren/Chen Yue's approved Remix/Random Wayfarer path, not Story selection. Rewards are non-power, bounded and never require co-op/purchase/hero/costume; ineligible clears retain access only and Story Highlights stay off.
- **Confirmed:** Stage 7 provisions are localized Yunzhou Rice Bowl→Health, Mineral Hydration Drink→Defense, Cocoa-Nut Bar→Attack and one Cooperative Meal Box→balanced. Medium solo has two each common/one box, co-op three each/one shared box, placed in authorized safe Office/Freight/Forum/pre-boss areas. Easy adds one each; Hard/Extreme/Ultimate remove one/two/three commons; box remains. Branches give none, evidence/cargo confusion is forbidden, shared J/state applies, FULLPANTRY affects ordinary eligible containers only, and effects make no medical claims.
- **Confirmed:** Stage 7 retains Stage 6 canonical defaults and adds character-specific visual-only **Evidence and Witness-Protection Outfit / 证据与证人保护装 / Ausrüstung für Beweis- und Zeugenschutz** and **Yunzhou Cooperative Formal Attire / 云州合作社礼服 / Festkleidung der Yunzhou-Kooperative**. Designs cover five Story heroes plus unlocked Soren/Chen Yue/Reed Warden, preserve affiliation identity and forbid readable/spoiler/Compact marks or power/stat/style/hitbox changes. Soren/Chen defaults are free; alternatives remain purchases. First eligible clear reveals per-hero Store items, prior ownership persists, mode eligibility applies, generated cinematics default canonically, and free default evolution waits for Stage 8.
- **Confirmed:** Stage 7 Guide **Evidence, Witnesses, and Field Support / 证据、证人与现场支援 / Beweise, Zeugen und Feldunterstützung** covers all mission systems with post-clear Compact/leader detail. Academy **Ledger Protection Drill / 账簿保护训练 / Übung zum Schutz der Akten** uses synthetic lessons for erasure, transfer, robot, equipment, property and shared objectives plus post-clear Silas practice. Established practice formats, assisted/unassisted paths, Kael/My Combos and full EN/zh-Hans/DE/access inputs apply. Training transfers/grants nothing and never gates Story.
- **Confirmed:** Stage 7 adds no cheat. Shared hero codes retain narrow scopes; notably STRONGHANDS excludes shield/evidence/nodes/property/vehicles/channels, TRUSTYTOOLS Baton only, STEADYRIDE player cargo-bike Stability while preserving play, QUIETCROWD retains specialists/objectives/witnesses/ledgers/Silas/discoveries, FULLPANTRY ordinary food only and HOMECOMING exact anchor. SAFEPASSAGE is Not Applicable and cannot preserve graded states. Any code marks Cheat-Assisted with canon/Stage 8/difficulties/mandatory content/catalogue but no competitive/economy/achievement/prestige result; localized cards and co-op consent apply.
- **Confirmed:** `story.stage7.hidden_ledger` versions stage/combat/evidence/witness/robot/scoring/replay/localization plus asset/rules hashes and blocks co-op mismatch. Archive stores all configuration, evidence, Tomas, witness, vehicle, robot, equipment, property, branch, enemy, Silas, score, checkpoint, network and reward facts with separate ineligible history/Legacy compatibility; Story stores no Highlights. Baseline values may be labelled as Combat Lab candidates until validated, but the document must distinguish them explicitly from approved final data and may not describe ungenerated or unapproved artwork as final.
- **Remaining after specification:** Combat Lab numerical tuning, detailed move and encounter authoring, final character names and visual identities, final content catalogues, media production, localization production, balance validation, browser and network testing, and implementation planning remain required work. They refine or instantiate confirmed models rather than reopening them by default.

## 13. Specification review decisions (2026-10-21)

> **Restored on 2026-11-03.** The refinement copy of this file was started from an earlier version and did not contain this section. It is copied back unchanged from the repository version so the 2026-10-21 to 2026-10-31 decisions (review D1–D11, title, founders' Stage 2 kits, input prompts, portable build, save and continue) stay in force.

These decisions resolve the review in `docs/review/01-findings-and-decisions.md` and take precedence over any earlier conflicting line in this log or the specification.

- **Confirmed (D1):** The elder is an age-progressed likeness of the creator's photo, about 65–70 years old, keeping face shape, eyes, and metal-frame glasses, with grey hair, a short grey beard, and traditional elder clothing. Only a Codex-generated stylized illustration of the elder, never the raw photo, is used as a Google Flow ingredient or start frame.
- **Confirmed (D2):** Visual style is cel-shaded illustrated characters (clean ink outlines, flat colours with two to three shade tones) over painterly backgrounds.
- **Confirmed (D3):** The setting is a fictional Chinese-inspired river village in a timeless pre-industrial style. The outside sponsor brings foreign industrial technology, so later stages escalate visually toward black iron, firearms, and motorbikes as part of the story.
- **Confirmed (D4):** The Mountain Guardian is a man of about 35–40, broad and sturdy, in practical work clothes. The River Scout is a woman of about 20–24, lean and agile. Their Simplified Chinese names are **山神** (Mountain Guardian) and **河神** (River Scout), kept as culturally specific names (honorific titles given by the villagers). English and German both use the English names **Mountain Guardian** and **River Scout**; they are not translated into German.
- **Confirmed (D5):** Character animation uses key-pose limited animation: three to six cleaned poses per action, each generated as a single image from the approved model sheet, with smears, effect layers, and hit-stop. Frame data remains authored at 60 Hz independent of drawing count. This replaces the exact-frame-strip requirement of the Anchored Hybrid Sprite Pipeline; model sheets, shared scale, bottom-centre anchors, and human cleanup remain.
- **Confirmed (D6):** Default keyboard bindings are defined by physical key position (`KeyW/KeyA/KeyS/KeyD`, `KeyT`, `KeyY`, `KeyU`, `KeyG`, `KeyH`, `KeyJ`, `KeyB`). The interface displays the character printed by the active layout (for example `Z` on German QWERTZ, `Y` on US QWERTY), using the browser layout map where available, otherwise the first observed key press, plus a layout picker in first-launch setup.
- **Confirmed (D7):** Same-device co-op supports an optional Split Keyboard source: Player 1 uses the left cluster and Player 2 uses arrows plus numpad (with an `IJKL`-area laptop preset). Gamepads remain recommended, and the live tester includes a keyboard-ghosting check.
- **Confirmed (D8):** Defeat uses per-stage continues by difficulty (candidate Easy 5, Medium 3, Hard 2, Extreme 1, Ultimate 0). A defeat spends a continue and restores the latest checkpoint (Stage 1 checkpoints at beats 3, 5, and before the boss). When no continue remains, the stage restarts. In co-op a defeated hero is downed; the partner may revive by holding Interact nearby for about two seconds (interruptible), otherwise the downed hero returns at the next encounter boundary. A team continue is spent only when both heroes are down.
- **Confirmed (D9):** The vertical slice ships local-Wi-Fi co-op as deterministic delay-based lockstep (adaptive two- to four-frame input delay, redundant input packets, state hashes, snapshot resync, courtesy hold, synchronized pause, safe session end). Prediction with bounded rollback, the restricted survival proxy, host migration, and authority migration are delivered after the slice. The confirmed models are unchanged; only their delivery is phased.
- **Confirmed (D10):** Vertical-slice content boundary: the store contains one purchased Special, one directional Ultimate, and one outfit per founder plus two profile badges; difficulty provides Easy, Medium, and Hard behaviour profiles for all core enemies plus the Black-Iron Enforcer, while Extreme and Ultimate are playable with core-roster behaviour profiles and receive new variant art after the slice. Future heroes, mounts, and advanced weapons move to a separate expansion-seeds document.
- **Confirmed (D11):** The abstract Team Attack action defaults to physical key `KeyB` and a gamepad shoulder button. Inviter and confirming partner use the same action.
- **Confirmed:** `favicon.svg` is used only as the browser-tab icon. It is the creator's signature icon across projects and has no influence on the game's design, palette, emblem, or artwork.
- **Confirmed:** The game title is rendered by the interface as localized typography over text-free artwork.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-25):** The official title is **Fight For Peace** (capital F in "For") in English and German; German does not translate it. The Simplified Chinese title is **和平之战**. The interface therefore needs two title logos (Latin and Chinese), and the browser tab title follows the active language. Older documents that write "Fight for Peace" refer to the same title.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-25):** Stage 2 is specified in `2026-10-24-fight-for-peace-stage-2-specification.md` (decision log: `stage2_feature_story_line.md`; Flow prompt catalogues: `docs/art/stage2/`). Its impact on the Stage 1 slice plan is recorded in `docs/review/04-stage2-impact-review.md`. D3's "timeless pre-industrial" setting describes Stage 1; by Stage 2 the village itself has modernized (greenhouses, aquaponics, clinic, civil-protection motorbikes) as the confirmed consequence of the Stage 1 victory.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-25):** Campaign structure (placeholder until more stages exist): Stage 2 unlocks after Stage 1 has been completed once on the profile. One profile carries Credits, purchases, wardrobe, records and Museum entries across stages. When all stages exist, Story Mode plays them in the predefined stage order; a Stage Challenge mode (replaying individual stages) is added after Story Mode is finished and is not designed yet.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-25):** The founders get full Stage 2 content like the twins. Mounted kits, using the same slots as the twins (neutral `T`, directional `T`, Jump hop, `G` lean, `U` Special, `H` Ultimate): Mountain Guardian **Mason's Reach**, **Bank-Sweep Kick**, **Stone-Step Hop**, **Anchor Lean**, **Rampart Escort**, **Open Gate Convoy**; River Scout **Reed-Tap Pass**, **Current Kick**, **Rapids Hop**, **Eddy Lean**, **Riverline Overtake**, **Floodline Run**. Stage 2 store outfits (visual only): Mountain Guardian **Greenhouse Builder** and **Harvest Heritage**; River Scout **Channel Patrol** and **Lantern Festival**. Frame data and exact behaviour follow the Stage 2 mounted rules and are Deferred like the twins' values.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-25):** As for the founders (D4), English and German both use the English names **Wind Sentinel** and **Fire Warden**; Simplified Chinese uses **风神** and **火神**.
- **Confirmed:** Gameplay SFX start from open-source and freely licensed sources (for example CC0 libraries and open-source synthesis tools). Every sound is referenced through a logical asset identifier so any sound can later be replaced, for example by ElevenLabs output, without code changes.
- **Confirmed:** Development uses placeholder art, video, and audio from the start. Generated media replaces placeholders incrementally, asset by asset, after creator review. Reference prompts (style and model sheets, test clips) are approved before the full numbered Codex and Google Flow prompt lists are produced.
- **Confirmed:** The game is not hosted for now and runs on local machines. Hosting remains a later, separately evaluated step.
- **Confirmed:** Technical review: keep the Layered Phaser Architecture; use Preact with signals for the DOM interface; Cloudflare Pages and Workers remain the recommended hosting option when hosting is revisited; the roughly 9–13-month slice plan is accepted, with a playable build or browser screenshots delivered for creator testing after every milestone; implementation happens in this repository.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-21):** LAN co-op is built first: each player runs their own copy of the game, and the guest joins the host's local room server with its LAN IP, port and room code over a WebSocket transport behind a replaceable `Transport` interface. Online mode (deployed room service, WebRTC, online room codes, targeting Cloudflare Pages and Workers) follows later through the same room protocol.
- **Deferred:** Anti-piracy/licensing and paid-user authentication are added after the creator is satisfied with game quality. The platform layer keeps a `LicenseAdapter` seam (always licensed in development) so either can be added without changing gameplay code. Distribution platform remains undecided.
- **Confirmed:** Development targets Node.js 24.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-21, reference review):** The Mountain Guardian's sleeveless jacket is deep slate blue (reference `R-03_mountain_v2`) so heroes separate from the faction's rust, ochre and black iron. Approved references: R-01 village (environment style), R-02 elder v2, R-03 Mountain Guardian, R-04 River Scout, R-05 lineup, R-06 enemies, R-07 bridge v2, R-08 gameplay mockup. Character sprites are generated without shadows (the engine draws shadows), and stage floors use level, horizontally repeatable paving without a central vanishing point.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-29, review 06):** Every player-facing input prompt (Academy lessons, Guide examples, Move Lists, HUD hints, control cards, My Combos playback) names an abstract action and renders that player's current binding for their active device, or the default if never changed (for example `T` for Attack for Player 1 on keyboard). Prompts update live on rebinding, device switch and layout change. Localized text never contains a literal key name.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-29, review 06):** Gamepad defaults (standard mapping): move on left stick and d-pad; Attack West; Jump South; Special North; Grab/Interact East; Guard RB/R1; Ultimate RT/R2; Team Attack LB/L1 (resolving D11); optional Dash LT/L2; Pause Start/Options. Button glyphs follow the detected pad family (Xbox, PlayStation, Nintendo, generic) with a manual override in Settings. Kenney's CC0 Input Prompts glyphs are the placeholders, replaceable through logical asset IDs.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-29, review 06):** The portable build is `release/FightForPeace-<version>.zip` (the `release/` folder is not committed), produced by `pnpm release` and containing only the built game, a bundled local server, `start.bat`, `start.sh` and `start.command`. The start script checks for Node.js 24+ and otherwise downloads the official portable Node.js into the game folder once (SHA-256 verified); the server listens on `127.0.0.1` port 6006, or the next free port up to 6105, and opens the default browser. In the portable build, profiles are saved by the local server into `saves/` inside the game folder (same versioned transactional format, snapshots and `.ffpsave` export/import), so saves are independent of the port and browser and move with the folder; the plain browser build keeps IndexedDB.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-29, refines D7):** Split-keyboard Player 2 defaults are arrow keys for movement, `Numpad4` Attack, `Numpad5` Jump, `Numpad6` Special, `Numpad1` Guard, `Numpad2` Ultimate and `Numpad3` Grab/Interact, mirroring Player 1's `T Z U` / `G H J`. Team Attack is `Numpad0`. The laptop preset without a numpad is proposed in the M1 plan, because the `IJKL` area overlaps Player 1's `U`, `H` and `J`.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-31, save and continue):** Continue from a checkpoint is supported only for solo and same-device (hotseat) co-op. Local-Wi-Fi co-op has no continuable save: if a Wi-Fi session ends early (quit, crash, or an unrecoverable disconnect), each profile keeps only its already confirmed statistics and checkpoint credit, and the run is restarted through Play and the lobby. This refines the interruption lines in section 1 and the specification's interruption rule.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-31, save and continue):** There is no "quit and resume here" save. Quitting mid-stage, closing the game, or a PC crash in solo or hotseat resumes from the latest confirmed checkpoint (Stage 1: beats 3, 5 and before the boss; Stage 2: Checkpoints 1–4), using the atomic checkpoint saves already defined in section 9.
- **Confirmed (2026-10-31, save and continue):** Checkpoint saving is automatic; players never save manually.
- **Proposed (2026-10-31):** When a checkpoint is saved, a short non-blocking "Checkpoint saved" notice with an icon shows for about 2 seconds, localized and announced to screen readers. It is not shown in local-Wi-Fi co-op, where no continuable save is made.
